@@ -27,4 +63,65 @@ public class FuelBarrel : MonoBehaviour
             gameObject.SetActive(false);
         }
     }
+
+    private void PlayBarrelAudio()
+    {
+        AudioClip chosenClip = PickClipFromList();
+        if (chosenClip == null) return;
+
+        float vol = Mathf.Clamp01(soundVolume);
+        AudioMixerGroup targetMixer = mixerGroup != null
+            ? mixerGroup
+            : (barrelAudioSource != null ? barrelAudioSource.outputAudioMixerGroup : null);
+
+        // Al desactivar el barril con gameObject.SetActive(false), un AudioSource temporal en 2D evita cortes y atenuación por distancia.
+        GameObject tempObj = new GameObject("TempFuelBarrelAudio");
+        tempObj.transform.position = transform.position;
+
+        AudioSource tempSource = tempObj.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
+        tempSource.clip = chosenClip;
+        tempSource.volume = vol;
+        tempSource.spatialBlend = force2DSound ? 0f : 1f;
+        tempSource.playOnAwake = false;
+
+        if (targetMixer != null)
+        {
+            tempSource.outputAudioMixerGroup = targetMixer;
+        }
+
+        tempSource.Play();
+        Destroy(tempObj, Mathf.Max(0.1f, chosenClip.length + 0.15f));
+    }
+
+    private AudioClip PickClipFromList()
+    {
+        if (pickupClips == null || pickupClips.Count == 0) return null;
+
+        if (audioPlayMode == AudioPlayMode.Sequential)
+        {
+            for (int i = 0; i < pickupClips.Count; i++)
+            {
+                int idx = (nextSequentialIndex + i) % pickupClips.Count;
+                if (pickupClips[idx] != null)
+                {
+                    AudioClip selected = pickupClips[idx];
+                    nextSequentialIndex = (idx + 1) % pickupClips.Count;
+                    return selected;
+                }
+            }
+            return null;
+        }
+
+        List<AudioClip> validClips = new List<AudioClip>();
+        for (int i = 0; i < pickupClips.Count; i++)
+        {
+            if (pickupClips[i] != null)
+            {
+                validClips.Add(pickupClips[i]);
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validClips.Count == 0) return null;
+        return validClips[Random.Range(0, validClips.Count)];
+    }
 }

+using System;
+using System.Collections;
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class ObstacleBehavior : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    public enum ObstacleKind
+    {
+        AutoFromTag,
+        Unknown,
+        Oil,
+        Cone,
+        Barrier
+    }
+
+    public enum AudioPlayMode
+    {
+        Random,
+        Sequential
+    }
+
+    [Serializable]
+    public class ObstacleAudioSection
+    {
+        [Tooltip("AudioSource específico para este obstáculo (opcional; si queda vacío usa el de respaldo o un reproductor 2D temporal).")]
+        public AudioSource audioSource;
+
+        [Tooltip("Lista de varios audios posibles al chocar o pisar este obstáculo.")]
+        public List<AudioClip> clips = new List<AudioClip>();
+
+        [Tooltip("Elige un clip al azar o en orden secuencial.")]
+        public AudioPlayMode playMode = AudioPlayMode.Random;
+
+        [Range(0f, 1f)]
+        [Tooltip("Volumen de reproducción para este obstáculo (0 a 1).")]
+        public float volume = 1f;
+
+        [Tooltip("Si está activo, reproduce el sonido en 2D (sin atenuación por distancia ni 3D) para que se escuche claro y fuerte.")]
+        public bool force2DSound = true;
+
+        [HideInInspector]
+        public int nextSequentialIndex;
+    }
+
+    [Serializable]
+    public class OilSectionConfig
+    {
+        [Header("Comportamiento y Combustible (Máx. tanque: 100)")]
+        [Tooltip("Duración en segundos del resbalón/desaceleración al pisar el aceite.")]
+        public float slowDuration = 2.0f;
+
+        [Min(0f)]
+        [Tooltip("Cantidad de combustible que RESTA al pisar aceite. Si es 0, usa ReduceFuelFromOil() de KartFuelSystem.")]
+        public float fuelLossOverride = 0f;
+
+        [Tooltip("Oculta o desactiva el aceite tras pisarlo.")]
+        public bool hideAfterHit = false;
+
+        [Tooltip("Segundos de espera antes de ocultar/desactivar el aceite.")]
+        public float hideDelay = 0.25f;
+
+        [Header("Audios del Aceite (Varios)")]
+        [Tooltip("Si está activo, también llama a AudioManager.Instance.PlayOilSlip.")]
+        public bool useAudioManagerSlip = true;
+
+        public ObstacleAudioSection audio = new ObstacleAudioSection();
+    }
+
+    [Serializable]
+    public class ConeSectionConfig
+    {
+        [Header("Comportamiento y Combustible (Máx. tanque: 100)")]
+        [Tooltip("Duración en segundos del freno temporal al tocar un cono.")]
+        public float slowDuration = 1.6f;
+
+        [Range(0f, 1f)]
+        [Tooltip("Multiplicador de velocidad mientras dura el freno del cono.")]
+        public float speedMultiplier = 0.45f;
+
+        [Min(0f)]
+        [Tooltip("Cantidad de combustible que RESTA al chocar contra conos (0 = no resta).")]
+        public float fuelLoss = 0f;
+
+        [Header("Rotura Natural del Cono")]
+        [Tooltip("Si está activo, el cono se rompe y se destruye al impactar en vez de quedarse intacto.")]
+        public bool breakAndDestroyOnHit = true;
+
+        [Tooltip("Partículas o efecto visual opcional al romperse el cono.")]
+        public GameObject breakEffectPrefab;
+
+        [Tooltip("Objeto visual opcional de cono roto/aplastado dentro del prefab.")]
+        public GameObject brokenVisualObject;
+
+        [Tooltip("Segundos de espera antes de destruir el cono tras romperse (da margen al audio).")]
+        public float destroyDelay = 0.6f;
+
+        [Tooltip("Oculta o desactiva los conos tras chocar si Break And Destroy On Hit está desactivado.")]
+        public bool hideAfterHit = false;
+
+        [Tooltip("Segundos de espera antes de ocultar/desactivar los conos.")]
+        public float hideDelay = 0.15f;
+
+        [Header("Audios de los Conos (Varios)")]
+        [Tooltip("Si está activo, también llama a AudioManager.Instance.PlayOilSlip.")]
+        public bool useAudioManagerSlip = false;
+
+        public ObstacleAudioSection audio = new ObstacleAudioSection { volume = 1f, force2DSound = true };
+    }
+
+    [Serializable]
+    public class BarrierSectionConfig
+    {
+        [Header("Comportamiento y Combustible (Máx. tanque: 100)")]
+        [Tooltip("Duración de referencia del bloqueo en el carril sin bloquear el cambio lateral de carril.")]
+        public float blockDuration = 0.9f;
+
+        [Range(0f, 0.5f)]
+        [Tooltip("Multiplicador de referencia al chocar de frente contra la valla.")]
+        public float blockSpeedMultiplier = 0.05f;
+
+        [Min(0f)]
+        [Tooltip("Cantidad de combustible que RESTA al chocar contra la valla (0 = no resta).")]
+        public float fuelPenalty = 0f;
+
+        [Header("Audios de la Valla STOP (Varios)")]
+        public ObstacleAudioSection audio = new ObstacleAudioSection { volume = 1f, force2DSound = true };
+    }
+
+    [Header("Identificación General")]
+    [Tooltip("AutoFromTag detecta 'Oil', 'Cone' o 'Barrier' desde el Tag del objeto o de su raíz.")]
+    [SerializeField] private ObstacleKind obstacleKind = ObstacleKind.AutoFromTag;
+
+    [Tooltip("Aplica efectos de velocidad y combustible. Desactívalo si solo quieres manejar audio/ocultado aquí.")]
+    [SerializeField] private bool applyGameplayEffects = true;
+
+    [Tooltip("Evita que el mismo obstáculo dispare más de una vez en el mismo choque.")]
+    [SerializeField] private bool triggerOnlyOnce = true;
+
+    [Tooltip("AudioSource de respaldo si un obstáculo no tiene uno asignado en su sección.")]
+    [SerializeField] private AudioSource fallbackAudioSource;
+
+    [Header("1. Aceite (Oil) - Configuración y Audios")]
+    [SerializeField] private OilSectionConfig oilConfig = new OilSectionConfig();
+
+    [Header("2. Conos (Cone) - Configuración y Audios")]
+    [SerializeField] private ConeSectionConfig coneConfig = new ConeSectionConfig();
+
+    [Header("3. Valla STOP (Barrier) - Configuración y Audios")]
+    [SerializeField] private BarrierSectionConfig barrierConfig = new BarrierSectionConfig();
+
+    private bool hasTriggered;
+
+    private void OnEnable()
+    {
+        hasTriggered = false;
+    }
+
+    private ObstacleKind ResolveKind()
+    {
+        if (obstacleKind != ObstacleKind.AutoFromTag)
+        {
+            return obstacleKind;
+        }
+
+        string tagToCheck = gameObject.tag;
+
+        if (tagToCheck == "Untagged" && transform.root != null)
+        {
+            tagToCheck = transform.root.gameObject.tag;
+        }
+
+        switch (tagToCheck)
+        {
+            case "Oil":
+                return ObstacleKind.Oil;
+
+            case "Cone":
+                return ObstacleKind.Cone;
+
+            case "Barrier":
+                return ObstacleKind.Barrier;
+
+            default:
+                return ObstacleKind.Unknown;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
+    {
+        ObstacleKind kind = ResolveKind();
+        HandleContact(other.gameObject, kind);
+    }
+
+    private void OnCollisionEnter(Collision collision)
+    {
+        ObstacleKind kind = ResolveKind();
+        HandleContact(collision.gameObject, kind);
+    }
+
+    private void HandleContact(GameObject hitObject, ObstacleKind kind)
+    {
+        if (kind == ObstacleKind.Unknown ||
+            (triggerOnlyOnce && hasTriggered))
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        Transform hitRoot = hitObject.transform.root;
+
+        KartLaneController kart =
+            hitObject.GetComponentInParent<KartLaneController>();
+        if (kart == null && hitRoot != null)
+        {
+            kart = hitRoot.GetComponentInChildren<KartLaneController>();
+        }
+
+        KartFuelSystem fuelSystem =
+            hitObject.GetComponentInParent<KartFuelSystem>();
+        if (fuelSystem == null && hitRoot != null)
+        {
+            fuelSystem = hitRoot.GetComponentInChildren<KartFuelSystem>();
+        }
+
+        bool isKart =
+            hitObject.CompareTag("Player") ||
+            hitObject.CompareTag("CPU") ||
+            (hitRoot != null && (hitRoot.CompareTag("Player") || hitRoot.CompareTag("CPU")));
+
+        if (!isKart && kart == null && fuelSystem == null)
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        if (triggerOnlyOnce)
+        {
+            hasTriggered = true;
+        }
+
+        switch (kind)
+        {
+            case ObstacleKind.Oil:
+                PlaySectionAudio(oilConfig.audio, willRemoveOrDisable: oilConfig.hideAfterHit);
+
+                if (oilConfig.useAudioManagerSlip && AudioManager.Instance != null)
+                {
+                    AudioManager.Instance.PlayOilSlip(oilConfig.audio.volume);
+                }
+
+                if (applyGameplayEffects)
+                {
+                    if (kart != null)
+                    {
+                        kart.ApplyOilSlow(oilConfig.slowDuration);
+                    }
+
+                    if (fuelSystem != null)
+                    {
+                        float overrideLoss = Mathf.Abs(oilConfig.fuelLossOverride);
+                        if (overrideLoss > 0f)
+                        {
+                            fuelSystem.ConsumeFuel(overrideLoss);
+                        }
+                        else
+                        {
+                            fuelSystem.ReduceFuelFromOil();
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (oilConfig.hideAfterHit)
+                {
+                    StartCoroutine(HideOrDisableRoutine(oilConfig.hideDelay));
+                }
+                break;
+
+            case ObstacleKind.Cone:
+                bool coneWillVanish = coneConfig.breakAndDestroyOnHit || coneConfig.hideAfterHit;
+                PlaySectionAudio(coneConfig.audio, willRemoveOrDisable: coneWillVanish);
+
+                if (coneConfig.useAudioManagerSlip && AudioManager.Instance != null)
+                {
+                    AudioManager.Instance.PlayOilSlip(coneConfig.audio.volume);
+                }
+
+                if (applyGameplayEffects)
+                {
+                    if (kart != null)
+                    {
+                        kart.ApplyObstacleSlow(coneConfig.slowDuration, coneConfig.speedMultiplier);
+                    }
+
+                    float coneLoss = Mathf.Abs(coneConfig.fuelLoss);
+                    if (fuelSystem != null && coneLoss > 0f)
+                    {
+                        fuelSystem.ConsumeFuel(coneLoss);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (coneConfig.breakAndDestroyOnHit)
+                {
+                    StartCoroutine(BreakAndDestroyConeRoutine(coneConfig));
+                }
+                else if (coneConfig.hideAfterHit)
+                {
+                    StartCoroutine(HideOrDisableRoutine(coneConfig.hideDelay));
+                }
+                break;
+
+            case ObstacleKind.Barrier:
+                PlaySectionAudio(barrierConfig.audio, willRemoveOrDisable: false);
+
+                if (applyGameplayEffects)
+                {
+                    if (kart != null)
+                    {
+                        kart.ApplyBarrierBlock(barrierConfig.blockDuration, barrierConfig.blockSpeedMultiplier);
+                    }
+
+                    float barrierLoss = Mathf.Abs(barrierConfig.fuelPenalty);
+                    if (fuelSystem != null && barrierLoss > 0f)
+                    {
+                        fuelSystem.ConsumeFuel(barrierLoss);
+                    }
+                }
+                break;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void PlaySectionAudio(ObstacleAudioSection section, bool willRemoveOrDisable)
+    {
+        if (section == null || section.clips == null || section.clips.Count == 0)
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        AudioClip chosenClip = null;
+
+        if (section.playMode == AudioPlayMode.Sequential)
+        {
+            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < section.clips.Count; attempt++)
+            {
+                int idx = (section.nextSequentialIndex + attempt) % section.clips.Count;
+                if (section.clips[idx] != null)
+                {
+                    chosenClip = section.clips[idx];
+                    section.nextSequentialIndex = (idx + 1) % section.clips.Count;
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            List<AudioClip> validClips = new List<AudioClip>();
+            for (int i = 0; i < section.clips.Count; i++)
+            {
+                if (section.clips[i] != null)
+                {
+                    validClips.Add(section.clips[i]);
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (validClips.Count > 0)
+            {
+                chosenClip = validClips[UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, validClips.Count)];
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (chosenClip == null)
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        float vol = Mathf.Clamp01(section.volume);
+        AudioSource targetSource = section.audioSource != null ? section.audioSource : fallbackAudioSource;
+
+        if (willRemoveOrDisable || targetSource == null || !gameObject.activeInHierarchy)
+        {
+            PlayClipOnTempSource(
+                chosenClip,
+                vol,
+                section.force2DSound,
+                targetSource != null ? targetSource.outputAudioMixerGroup : null
+            );
+            return;
+        }
+
+        if (section.force2DSound)
+        {
+            targetSource.spatialBlend = 0f;
+        }
+
+        targetSource.PlayOneShot(chosenClip, vol);
+    }
+
+    private void PlayClipOnTempSource(
+        AudioClip clip,
+        float volume,
+        bool force2D,
+        UnityEngine.Audio.AudioMixerGroup mixerGroup)
+    {
+        if (clip == null) return;
+
+        GameObject tempObj = new GameObject("TempObstacleAudio");
+        tempObj.transform.position = transform.position;
+
+        AudioSource tempSource = tempObj.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
+        tempSource.clip = clip;
+        tempSource.volume = Mathf.Clamp01(volume);
+        tempSource.spatialBlend = force2D ? 0f : 1f;
+        tempSource.playOnAwake = false;
+
+        if (mixerGroup != null)
+        {
+            tempSource.outputAudioMixerGroup = mixerGroup;
+        }
+
+        tempSource.Play();
+        Destroy(tempObj, Mathf.Max(0.1f, clip.length + 0.15f));
+    }
+
+    private IEnumerator BreakAndDestroyConeRoutine(ConeSectionConfig config)
+    {
+        if (config.breakEffectPrefab != null)
+        {
+            Instantiate(config.breakEffectPrefab, transform.position, Quaternion.identity);
+        }
+
+        foreach (Collider col in GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>())
+        {
+            col.enabled = false;
+        }
+
+        if (config.brokenVisualObject != null)
+        {
+            foreach (Renderer rend in GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
+            {
+                rend.enabled = false;
+            }
+
+            config.brokenVisualObject.SetActive(true);
+            foreach (Renderer brokenRend in config.brokenVisualObject.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
+            {
+                brokenRend.enabled = true;
+            }
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            foreach (Renderer rend in GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
+            {
+                rend.enabled = false;
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (config.destroyDelay > 0f)
+        {
+            yield return new WaitForSeconds(config.destroyDelay);
+        }
+
+        Destroy(gameObject);
+    }
+
+    private IEnumerator HideOrDisableRoutine(float delay)
+    {
+        if (ResolveKind() == ObstacleKind.Barrier)
+        {
+            yield break;
+        }
+
+        if (delay > 0f)
+        {
+            yield return new WaitForSeconds(delay);
+        }
+
+        foreach (Renderer rend in GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
+        {
+            rend.enabled = false;
+        }
+
+        foreach (Collider col in GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>())
+        {
+            col.enabled = false;
+        }
+
+        gameObject.SetActive(false);
+    }
+}

commit 1ecb83aae5b4f9194668cbcf007f4d24c03ef031
Author:     David Silva <davidalfonsosilvaarenas9@gmail.com>
AuthorDate: 2026-11-22 01:38:01 -0500
Commit:     David Silva <davidalfonsosilvaarenas9@gmail.com>
CommitDate: 2026-11-22 01:38:01 -0500

    Equilibrio del turbo de la CPU en nivel 1
    
    Se ajustó el sistema de turbo de la CPU para equilibrar las reglas con el jugador.
    
    - Límite de 3 turbos y duración de 2 segundos.
    - Comprobación del combustible mínimo antes de activar el turbo.
    - Espera progresiva según el avance de la carrera.
    - Consumo adicional de combustible durante el turbo.
    - Reinicio de temporizadores, estadísticas y audio entre carreras.
    - Pruebas funcionales realizadas en Gameplay sin errores de compilación.

    - Optimización de la velocidad y la selección de carriles.
    - Mejora de la detección de bidones, manchas de aceite y obstáculos.

    - Corrección de la etiqueta Obstacle para eliminar errores de ejecución.
    - Desactivación del generador duplicado de bidones.
    - Validación del funcionamiento de la CPU en Gameplay.

+    {

+            GameObject item = spawnedItems[i];

+    private void ShowBoostRemainingNotification()
+    {
+        if (!enableBoostNotification || boostNotificationText == null) return;

+        string message;
+        float extraHold = 0f;
+
+        if (usesRemaining == 1)
+        {

+        }

+    }

+    private IEnumerator TypewriterNotificationRoutine(string fullMessage, float extraHold)
+    {
+        SetBoostNotificationVisible(true);
+        boostNotificationText.text = string.Empty;
+
+        float charDelay = Mathf.Max(0.005f, typewriterCharDelay);
+        for (int i = 0; i < fullMessage.Length; i++)
+        {
+            boostNotificationText.text += fullMessage[i];
+            yield return new WaitForSeconds(charDelay);
+        }
+
+        float totalHold = Mathf.Max(0.2f, holdAfterTypingSeconds + extraHold);
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(totalHold);
+
+        SetBoostNotificationVisible(false);
+        notificationCoroutine = null;
+    }

+    private void BuildShuffledLanes()
+    {
+        lanePool.Clear();
+        for (int i = 0; i < laneXPositions.Length; i++)

+    private float RollNextWaitDuration()

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Tutorial/TutorialSlidesController.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Tutorial/TutorialSlidesController.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..faa3df3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Tutorial/TutorialSlidesController.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,119 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.UI;
+using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
+
+public class TutorialSlidesController : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Diapositivas del Tutorial (Opcional si los sprites van en cada Grupo)")]
+    [SerializeField] private Image slideDisplayImage;
+    [SerializeField] private Sprite[] tutorialSlides;
+
+    [Header("Grupos de Contenido por Página (3 Objetos)")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject[] pageTextGroups;
+
+    [Header("Botones sobre las imágenes")]
+    [SerializeField] private Button prevButton;
+    [SerializeField] private Button nextButton;
+    [SerializeField] private Button mainActionButton;
+
+    [Header("Conexión con la Práctica")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject visualTutorialPanel;
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject hudPlayToRestore;
+
+    private int currentPage = 0;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        Time.timeScale = 0f;
+
+        if (hudPlayToRestore != null)
+            hudPlayToRestore.SetActive(false);
+
+        if (prevButton != null) prevButton.onClick.AddListener(PreviousPage);
+        if (nextButton != null) nextButton.onClick.AddListener(NextPage);
+        if (mainActionButton != null) mainActionButton.onClick.AddListener(HandleMainButton);
+
+        UpdateSlideUI();
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        if (visualTutorialPanel == null || !visualTutorialPanel.activeInHierarchy) return;
+        if (Keyboard.current == null) return;
+
+        if (Keyboard.current.rightArrowKey.wasPressedThisFrame || Keyboard.current.enterKey.wasPressedThisFrame)
+        {
+            HandleMainButton();
+        }
+        else if (Keyboard.current.leftArrowKey.wasPressedThisFrame)
+        {
+            PreviousPage();
+        }
+    }
+
+    public void NextPage()
+    {
+        if (currentPage < pageTextGroups.Length - 1)
+        {
+            currentPage++;
+            UpdateSlideUI();
+        }
+    }
+
+    public void PreviousPage()
+    {
+        if (currentPage > 0)
+        {
+            currentPage--;
+            UpdateSlideUI();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void HandleMainButton()
+    {
+        if (currentPage < pageTextGroups.Length - 1)
+        {
+            NextPage();
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            StartPracticeMode();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void UpdateSlideUI()
+    {
+        if (tutorialSlides != null && tutorialSlides.Length > currentPage && tutorialSlides[currentPage] != null && slideDisplayImage != null)
+        {
+            slideDisplayImage.sprite = tutorialSlides[currentPage];
+        }
+
+        if (pageTextGroups != null)
+        {
+            for (int i = 0; i < pageTextGroups.Length; i++)
+            {
+                if (pageTextGroups[i] != null)
+                {
+                    pageTextGroups[i].SetActive(i == currentPage);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (prevButton != null)
+            prevButton.gameObject.SetActive(currentPage > 0);
+
+        if (nextButton != null)
+            nextButton.gameObject.SetActive(currentPage < pageTextGroups.Length - 1);
+    }
+
+    public void StartPracticeMode()
+    {
+        Time.timeScale = 1f;
+
+        if (hudPlayToRestore != null)
+            hudPlayToRestore.SetActive(true);
+
+        if (visualTutorialPanel != null)
+            visualTutorialPanel.SetActive(false);
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/TrackItemSpawner.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/TrackItemSpawner.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..01ece14
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/TrackItemSpawner.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,164 @@
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class TrackItemSpawner : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Prefabs (Arrastrar desde Assets/Prefabs)")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject fuelDrumPrefab;
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject oilSpillPrefab;
+
+    [Header("Configuración de los 4 Carriles (Coordenadas X)")]
+    [SerializeField] private float[] laneXPositions = new float[] { -104.9f, -102.9f, -100.9f, -98.9f };
+
+    [Header("Alturas Y de aparición")]
+    [SerializeField] private float fuelHeightY = 463.896f;
+    [SerializeField] private float oilHeightY = 463.7f;
+
+    [Header("Parámetros de Generación en Pista")]
+    [Tooltip("Distancia por delante del jugador donde van apareciendo los objetos.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float spawnAheadDistance = 30f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Separación base en Z entre cada oleada.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float spawnIntervalZ = 9f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Variación máxima en Z (+/-) para que los objetos salgan escalonados y no en línea recta.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float zStaggerRange = 3.2f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Posición Z máxima hasta donde se pueden generar objetos (antes de la meta).")]
+    [SerializeField] private float maxSpawnZ = 712f;
+
+    [Header("Dificultad y Probabilidades")]
+    [Range(0f, 1f)]
+    [SerializeField] private float fuelSpawnChance = 0.55f;
+
+    [Range(0f, 1f)]
+    [SerializeField] private float oilSpawnChance = 0.90f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Mínimo de manchas de aceite por oleada.")]
+    [Range(1, 3)]
+    [SerializeField] private int minOilPerRow = 1;
+
+    [Tooltip("Máximo de manchas de aceite por oleada (nunca 4 para que siempre haya paso).")]
+    [Range(1, 3)]
+    [SerializeField] private int maxOilPerRow = 3;
+
+    [Tooltip("Cantidad mínima de carriles libres garantizados en cada oleada.")]
+    [Range(1, 2)]
+    [SerializeField] private int guaranteedFreeLanes = 1;
+
+    private Transform playerTransform;
+    private Transform cpuTransform;
+    private float startZ;
+    private float nextSpawnZ;
+    private bool isInitialized = false;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        GameObject player = GameObject.FindGameObjectWithTag("Player");
+        if (player != null) playerTransform = player.transform;
+
+        GameObject cpu = GameObject.FindGameObjectWithTag("CPU");
+        if (cpu != null) cpuTransform = cpu.transform;
+
+        startZ = GetLeadingKartZ();
+        nextSpawnZ = startZ + 10f;
+        isInitialized = true;
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        if (!isInitialized || GameManager.Instance == null) return;
+
+        if (GameManager.Instance.gameState == GameManager.GameState.Waiting ||
+            GameManager.Instance.gameState == GameManager.GameState.Finished)
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        float leadingZ = GetLeadingKartZ();
+
+        while (leadingZ + spawnAheadDistance >= nextSpawnZ && nextSpawnZ <= maxSpawnZ)
+        {
+            SpawnWaveAt(nextSpawnZ);
+            nextSpawnZ += spawnIntervalZ;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private float GetLeadingKartZ()
+    {
+        float maxZ = float.MinValue;
+
+        if (playerTransform != null && playerTransform.gameObject.activeInHierarchy)
+            maxZ = Mathf.Max(maxZ, playerTransform.position.z);
+
+        if (cpuTransform != null && cpuTransform.gameObject.activeInHierarchy)
+            maxZ = Mathf.Max(maxZ, cpuTransform.position.z);
+
+        return (maxZ == float.MinValue) ? 0f : maxZ;
+    }
+
+    private float GetStaggeredZ(float baseZ)
+    {
+        // Desplaza cada ítem hacia adelante o atrás dentro de la oleada sin pasar la meta
+        float randomOffset = Random.Range(-zStaggerRange, zStaggerRange);
+        return Mathf.Clamp(baseZ + randomOffset, startZ + 8f, maxSpawnZ);
+    }
+
+    private void SpawnWaveAt(float baseZ)
+    {
+        if (laneXPositions == null || laneXPositions.Length == 0) return;
+
+        List<int> availableLanes = new List<int>();
+        for (int i = 0; i < laneXPositions.Length; i++)
+        {
+            availableLanes.Add(i);
+        }
+
+        // Reserva carriles libres de aceite para asegurar siempre un camino pasable
+        int safeLanesCount = Mathf.Clamp(guaranteedFreeLanes, 1, laneXPositions.Length - 1);
+        List<int> safeLanes = new List<int>();
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < safeLanesCount; i++)
+        {
+            int idx = Random.Range(0, availableLanes.Count);
+            safeLanes.Add(availableLanes[idx]);
+            availableLanes.RemoveAt(idx);
+        }
+
+        // 1. Bidón de gasolina en un carril seguro, con su propia altura Z escalonada
+        if (fuelDrumPrefab != null && Random.value <= fuelSpawnChance && safeLanes.Count > 0)
+        {
+            int chosenSafeLane = safeLanes[Random.Range(0, safeLanes.Count)];
+            float staggeredZ = GetStaggeredZ(baseZ);
+            Vector3 fuelPos = new Vector3(laneXPositions[chosenSafeLane], fuelHeightY, staggeredZ);
+            Instantiate(fuelDrumPrefab, fuelPos, fuelDrumPrefab.transform.rotation, transform);
+        }
+
+        // 2. Manchas de aceite en los carriles restantes, cada una en una posición Z distinta (escalonadas)
+        if (oilSpillPrefab != null && Random.value <= oilSpawnChance && availableLanes.Count > 0)
+        {
+            float trackProgress = Mathf.InverseLerp(startZ, maxSpawnZ, baseZ);
+            int dynamicMin = (trackProgress > 0.5f) ? Mathf.Min(2, maxOilPerRow) : minOilPerRow;
+            int oilCount = Random.Range(dynamicMin, maxOilPerRow + 1);
+            oilCount = Mathf.Min(oilCount, availableLanes.Count);
+
+            // Reparte desplazamientos distintos para que las manchas de la misma oleada no queden en línea
+            float step = (oilCount > 1) ? (zStaggerRange * 2f) / (oilCount - 1) : 0f;
+
+            for (int i = 0; i < oilCount; i++)
+            {
+                int randomIndex = Random.Range(0, availableLanes.Count);
+                int chosenLane = availableLanes[randomIndex];
+                availableLanes.RemoveAt(randomIndex);
+
+                float structuredOffset = (oilCount > 1)
+                    ? (-zStaggerRange + (step * i)) + Random.Range(-0.6f, 0.6f)
+                    : Random.Range(-zStaggerRange, zStaggerRange);
+
+                float oilZ = Mathf.Clamp(baseZ + structuredOffset, startZ + 8f, maxSpawnZ);
+                Vector3 oilPos = new Vector3(laneXPositions[chosenLane], oilHeightY, oilZ);
+                Instantiate(oilSpillPrefab, oilPos, oilSpillPrefab.transform.rotation, transform);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Managers/AudioManager.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Managers/AudioManager.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a6033b4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Managers/AudioManager.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,135 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.Audio;
+using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
+
+public class AudioManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    public static AudioManager Instance;
+
+    [Header("AudioMixer Principal")]
+    [SerializeField] private AudioMixer mainAudioMixer;
+    [SerializeField] private string musicExposedParam = "VolumeMaster";
+    [SerializeField] private AudioMixerGroup musicMixerGroup;
+
+    [SerializeField] private string sfxExposedParam = "SFXVolume";
+    [SerializeField] private AudioMixerGroup sfxMixerGroup;
+
+    [Header("Música (dejar en None si ya la reproduce MusicManager)")]
+    [SerializeField] private AudioClip mainMenuMusic;
+    [SerializeField] private AudioClip gameplayMusic;
+    [SerializeField] private string gameplaySceneName = "Gameplay";
+    [Range(0f, 1f)][SerializeField] private float musicBaseVolume = 0.7f;
+
+    [Header("Efectos (SFX)")]
+    [SerializeField] private AudioClip fuelPickupClip;
+    [SerializeField] private AudioClip oilSlipClip;
+    [SerializeField] private AudioClip turboClip;
+
+    private AudioSource musicSource;
+    private AudioSource sfxSource;
+
+    private const string MUSIC_PREF_KEY = "MusicVolumeDB";
+    private const string SFX_PREF_KEY = "SFXVolumeDB";
+
+    private void Awake()
+    {
+        if (Instance == null)
+        {
+            Instance = this;
+            DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            Destroy(gameObject);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        musicSource = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
+        musicSource.loop = true;
+        musicSource.playOnAwake = false;
+        musicSource.volume = musicBaseVolume;
+        musicSource.outputAudioMixerGroup = musicMixerGroup;
+
+        sfxSource = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
+        sfxSource.loop = false;
+        sfxSource.playOnAwake = false;
+        sfxSource.outputAudioMixerGroup = sfxMixerGroup;
+    }
+
+    private void OnEnable()
+    {
+        SceneManager.sceneLoaded += OnSceneLoaded;
+    }
+
+    private void OnDisable()
+    {
+        SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnSceneLoaded;
+    }
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        float savedMusic = PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(MUSIC_PREF_KEY, 0f);
+        float savedSfx = PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(SFX_PREF_KEY, 0f);
+
+        SetMusicVolumeDB(savedMusic);
+        SetSFXVolumeDB(savedSfx);
+
+        PlayMusicForScene(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name);
+    }
+
+    private void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
+    {
+        PlayMusicForScene(scene.name);
+    }
+
+    public void PlayMusicForScene(string sceneName)
+    {
+        AudioClip target = (sceneName == gameplaySceneName) ? gameplayMusic : mainMenuMusic;
+
+        if (target == null)
+        {
+            if (musicSource.isPlaying) musicSource.Stop();
+            return;
+        }
+
+        if (musicSource.clip == target && musicSource.isPlaying) return;
+
+        musicSource.clip = target;
+        musicSource.volume = musicBaseVolume;
+        musicSource.outputAudioMixerGroup = musicMixerGroup;
+        musicSource.Play();
+    }
+
+    public void PlaySFX(AudioClip clip, float volumeScale = 1f)
+    {
+        if (clip == null || sfxSource == null) return;
+        sfxSource.outputAudioMixerGroup = sfxMixerGroup;
+        sfxSource.PlayOneShot(clip, volumeScale);
+    }
+
+    public void PlayFuelPickup(float volumeScale = 1f) => PlaySFX(fuelPickupClip, volumeScale);
+    public void PlayOilSlip(float volumeScale = 1f) => PlaySFX(oilSlipClip, volumeScale);
+    public void PlayTurbo(float volumeScale = 1f) => PlaySFX(turboClip, volumeScale);
+
+    public void SetMusicVolumeDB(float dbValue)
+    {
+        if (mainAudioMixer != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(musicExposedParam))
+        {
+            mainAudioMixer.SetFloat(musicExposedParam, dbValue);
+        }
+
+        PlayerPrefs.SetFloat(MUSIC_PREF_KEY, dbValue);
+        PlayerPrefs.Save();
+    }
+
+    public void SetSFXVolumeDB(float dbValue)
+    {
+        if (mainAudioMixer != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(sfxExposedParam))
+        {
+            mainAudioMixer.SetFloat(sfxExposedParam, dbValue);
+        }
+
+        PlayerPrefs.SetFloat(SFX_PREF_KEY, dbValue);
+        PlayerPrefs.Save();
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Managers/PauseMenuController.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Managers/PauseMenuController.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9a8a90d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Managers/PauseMenuController.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.UI;
+using UnityEngine.Audio;
+using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
+
+public class PauseMenuController : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("UI Pausa y HUD")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject pauseMenuPanel;
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject hudPauseButton;
+
+    [Header("Audio (igual que en Main Menu)")]
+    [SerializeField] private AudioMixer musicMixer;
+    [SerializeField] private string musicExposedParam = "VolumeMaster"; // Pon aquí el nombre exacto que usa tu Main Menu
+    [SerializeField] private AudioSource fallbackMusicSource;         // Opcional: si tu música va por AudioSource directo
+
+    [SerializeField] private AudioMixer sfxMixer;
+    [SerializeField] private string sfxExposedParam = "SFXVolume";     // Nombre expuesto en el grupo SFX
+
+    [Header("Sliders")]
+    [SerializeField] private Slider musicSlider;
+    [SerializeField] private Slider sfxSlider;
+
+    [Header("Escena Menú")]
+    [SerializeField] private string mainMenuSceneName = "MainMenu";
+
+    private const string MUSIC_PREF_KEY = "MusicVolume";
+    private const string SFX_PREF_KEY = "SFXVolume";
+    private bool isPaused = false;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        Time.timeScale = 1f;
+        isPaused = false;
+
+        if (pauseMenuPanel != null) pauseMenuPanel.SetActive(false);
+        if (hudPauseButton != null) hudPauseButton.SetActive(true);
+
+        float savedMusic = PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(MUSIC_PREF_KEY, 0.75f);
+        float savedSfx = PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(SFX_PREF_KEY, 0.75f);
+
+        if (musicSlider != null)
+        {
+            musicSlider.minValue = 0.0001f;
+            musicSlider.maxValue = 1f;
+            musicSlider.SetValueWithoutNotify(savedMusic);
+            musicSlider.onValueChanged.RemoveAllListeners();
+            musicSlider.onValueChanged.AddListener(SetMusicVolume);
+        }
+        ApplyMusicVolume(savedMusic);
+
+        if (sfxSlider != null)
+        {
+            sfxSlider.minValue = 0.0001f;
+            sfxSlider.maxValue = 1f;
+            sfxSlider.SetValueWithoutNotify(savedSfx);
+            sfxSlider.onValueChanged.RemoveAllListeners();
+            sfxSlider.onValueChanged.AddListener(SetSFXVolume);
+        }
+        ApplySFXVolume(savedSfx);
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape))
+        {
+            if (isPaused) ResumeGame();
+            else PauseGame();
+        }
+    }
+
+    public void PauseGame()
+    {
+        isPaused = true;
+        Time.timeScale = 0f;
+        if (pauseMenuPanel != null) pauseMenuPanel.SetActive(true);
+        if (hudPauseButton != null) hudPauseButton.SetActive(false);
+        Cursor.lockState = CursorLockMode.None;
+        Cursor.visible = true;
+    }
+
+    public void ResumeGame()
+    {
+        isPaused = false;
+        Time.timeScale = 1f;
+        if (pauseMenuPanel != null) pauseMenuPanel.SetActive(false);
+        if (hudPauseButton != null) hudPauseButton.SetActive(true);
+    }
+
+    public void RestartLevel()
+    {
+        isPaused = false;
+        Time.timeScale = 1f;
+        Scene currentScene = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
+        SceneManager.LoadScene(currentScene.buildIndex);
+    }
+
+    public void GoToMainMenu()
+    {
+        isPaused = false;
+        Time.timeScale = 1f;
+        SceneManager.LoadScene(mainMenuSceneName);
+    }
+
+    public void SetMusicVolume(float value)
+    {
+        ApplyMusicVolume(value);
+        PlayerPrefs.SetFloat(MUSIC_PREF_KEY, value);
+        PlayerPrefs.Save();
+    }
+
+    public void SetSFXVolume(float value)
+    {
+        ApplySFXVolume(value);
+        PlayerPrefs.SetFloat(SFX_PREF_KEY, value);
+        PlayerPrefs.Save();
+    }
+
+    private void ApplyMusicVolume(float value)
+    {
+        float clamped = Mathf.Clamp(value, 0.0001f, 1f);
+        if (musicMixer != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(musicExposedParam))
+        {
+            musicMixer.SetFloat(musicExposedParam, Mathf.Log10(clamped) * 20f);
+        }
+        if (fallbackMusicSource != null)
+        {
+            fallbackMusicSource.volume = value;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void ApplySFXVolume(float value)
+    {
+        float clamped = Mathf.Clamp(value, 0.0001f, 1f);
+        if (sfxMixer != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(sfxExposedParam))
+        {
+            sfxMixer.SetFloat(sfxExposedParam, Mathf.Log10(clamped) * 20f);
+        }
+    }
+}

+    }

+    private bool HasMissedFuelBarrel()
+    {
+        if (tutorialFuelBarrel == null ||
+            playerController == null ||
+            !tutorialFuelBarrel.activeInHierarchy)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        float playerZ = playerController.transform.position.z;
+        float barrelZ = tutorialFuelBarrel.transform.position.z;
+
+        return playerZ > barrelZ + 3f;
+    }

+using UnityEngine;
+using TMPro;
+using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
+
+public class NameInputManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("UI Reference")]
+    [SerializeField] private TMP_InputField inputNombre;
+
+    // Esta función la llamaremos desde el botón "Continuar"
+    public void ConfirmName()
+    {
+        // Si el jugador escribió algo, lo guardamos
+        if (inputNombre != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(inputNombre.text))
+        {
+            GameData.PlayerName = inputNombre.text;
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            // Si lo deja vacío, ponemos un nombre por defecto
+            GameData.PlayerName = "Jugador 1";
+        }
+
+        Debug.Log("Nombre guardado: " + GameData.PlayerName);
+
+        // Cargamos la escena de selección de personaje
+        SceneManager.LoadScene("CharacterSelection");
+    }
+
+    // Esta función la llamaremos desde el botón "Salir"
+    public void ExitToMenu()
+    {
+        SceneManager.LoadScene("MainMenu");
+    }
+}

@@ -98,6 +148,78 @@ public class GameManager : MonoBehaviour
     }
 
 
+    public void ContinueInstructions()
+    {
+        if (gameState != GameState.Waiting)
+            return;
+
+        currentInstruction++;
+
+        if (previousButton != null)
+            previousButton.interactable = currentInstruction > 0;
+
+        if (currentInstruction < tutorialMessages.Length)
+        {
+            instructionText.text = tutorialMessages[currentInstruction];
+
+            if (progressText != null)
+            {
+                progressText.text =
+                    (currentInstruction + 1) + " de " + tutorialMessages.Length;
+            }
+
+            if (continueButtonText != null)
+            {
+                continueButtonText.text =
+                    currentInstruction == tutorialMessages.Length - 1
+                    ? "COMENZAR"
+                    : "CONTINUAR";
+            }
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            continueInstructions = true;
+        }
+    }
+
+
+    public void PreviousInstruction()
+    {
+        if (gameState != GameState.Waiting)
+            return;
+
+        if (currentInstruction <= 0)
+            return;
+
+        currentInstruction--;
+
+        if (previousButton != null)
+            previousButton.interactable = currentInstruction > 0;
+
+        instructionText.text = tutorialMessages[currentInstruction];
+
+        if (progressText != null)
+        {
+            progressText.text =
+                (currentInstruction + 1) + " de " + tutorialMessages.Length;
+        }
+
+        if (continueButtonText != null)
+        {
+            continueButtonText.text = "CONTINUAR";
+        }
+    }
+
+
+    public void SkipTutorial()
+    {
+        if (gameState != GameState.Waiting)
+            return;
+
+        continueInstructions = true;
+    }
+

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Tutorial/TutorialManager.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Tutorial/TutorialManager.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ff27b80
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Tutorial/TutorialManager.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,398 @@
+﻿
+using System.Collections;
+using UnityEngine;
+using TMPro;
+using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
+
+public class TutorialManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Player")]
+    [SerializeField] private KartLaneController playerController;
+
+    [Header("Tutorial UI")]
+    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI instructionText;
+    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI progressText;
+
+    [Header("Exercise Settings")]
+    [SerializeField] private float requiredLaneDistance = 1.8f;
+    [SerializeField] private float messageDelay = 1.5f;
+
+    [Header("Fuel Exercise")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject tutorialFuelBarrel;
+    [SerializeField] private KartFuelSystem playerFuelSystem;
+
+    [Header("Tutorial Movement")]
+    [SerializeField] private float tutorialForwardSpeed = 4f;
+
+    [Header("Oil Exercise")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject tutorialOilSpill;
+    [SerializeField] private TutorialOilDetector oilDetector;
+
+    [Header("Completion UI")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject completionPanel;
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject instructionsPanel;
+
+    private float initialX;
+    private int currentExercise = 0;
+    private bool correctKeyPressed = false;
+    private bool transitioning = false;
+    private float fuelBeforeCollection;
+    private Vector3 oilExerciseStartPosition;
+    private Quaternion oilExerciseStartRotation;
+
+    private bool turboExerciseCompleted = false;
+
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        if (playerController == null)
+        {
+            Debug.LogError("Tutorial: Player controller is missing.");
+            enabled = false;
+            return;
+        }
+
+
+        playerController.canMove = true;
+        playerController.canUseTurbo = false;
+        playerController.SetForwardSpeed(0f);
+
+        StartExercise(0);
+
+    }
+
+    private void StartExercise(int exercise)
+    {
+        currentExercise = exercise;
+        initialX = playerController.transform.position.x;
+        correctKeyPressed = false;
+        transitioning = false;
+
+        if (exercise == 0)
+        {
+            UpdateUI(
+                "Presiona la flecha DERECHA →",
+                "PASO 1 DE 5"
+            );
+        }
+        else if (exercise == 1)
+        {
+            UpdateUI(
+                "Presiona la flecha IZQUIERDA ←",
+                "PASO 2 DE 5"
+            );
+        }
+
+        else if (exercise == 2)
+        {
+            UpdateUI(
+                "¡Recoge el bidón de combustible!",
+                "PASO 3 DE 5"
+            );
+
+            if (playerFuelSystem != null)
+            {
+                fuelBeforeCollection = playerFuelSystem.GetFuelNormalized();
+            }
+
+            if (tutorialFuelBarrel != null)
+            {
+                tutorialFuelBarrel.SetActive(true);
+            }
+
+            playerController.canMove = true;
+            playerController.SetForwardSpeed(tutorialForwardSpeed);
+        }
+
+        else if (exercise == 3)
+        {
+
+            oilExerciseStartPosition = playerController.transform.position;
+            oilExerciseStartRotation = playerController.transform.rotation;
+
+            UpdateUI(
+                "¡Esquiva la mancha de aceite cambiando de carril!",
+                "PASO 4 DE 5"
+            );
+
+            if (oilDetector != null)
+            {
+                oilDetector.ResetDetection();
+            }
+
+            if (tutorialOilSpill != null)
+            {
+                tutorialOilSpill.SetActive(true);
+            }
+
+            playerController.canMove = true;
+            playerController.SetForwardSpeed(tutorialForwardSpeed);
+        }
+
+
+        else if (exercise == 4)
+        {
+            UpdateUI(
+                "¡Presiona la flecha ARRIBA para activar el PROPULSOR!",
+                "PASO 5 DE 5"
+            );
+
+
+            turboExerciseCompleted = false;
+
+            playerController.canUseTurbo = true;
+            playerController.canMove = true;
+            playerController.SetForwardSpeed(tutorialForwardSpeed);
+
+        }
+
+
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        if (transitioning || currentExercise >= 5)
+            return;
+
+        if (currentExercise == 0)
+        {
+            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.RightArrow))
+                correctKeyPressed = true;
+
+            float distance =
+                playerController.transform.position.x - initialX;
+
+            if (correctKeyPressed &&
+                distance >= requiredLaneDistance)
+            {
+                StartCoroutine(CompleteExercise());
+            }
+        }
+        else if (currentExercise == 1)
+        {
+            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.LeftArrow))
+                correctKeyPressed = true;
+
+            float distance =
+                initialX - playerController.transform.position.x;
+
+            if (correctKeyPressed &&
+                distance >= requiredLaneDistance)
+            {
+                StartCoroutine(CompleteExercise());
+            }
+        }
+        else if (currentExercise == 2)
+        {
+            if (playerFuelSystem != null)
+            {
+                float currentFuel =
+                    playerFuelSystem.GetFuelNormalized();
+
+                if (currentFuel > fuelBeforeCollection + 0.001f)
+                {
+                    StartCoroutine(CompleteFuelExercise());
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+
+        else if (currentExercise == 3)
+        {
+            if (tutorialOilSpill == null || oilDetector == null)
+                return;
+
+            if (oilDetector.PlayerTouchedOil)
+            {
+                StartCoroutine(CompleteOilExercise(false));
+                return;
+            }
+
+            float playerZ = playerController.transform.position.z;
+            float oilZ = tutorialOilSpill.transform.position.z;
+
+            if (playerZ >= oilZ + 3f)
+            {
+                StartCoroutine(CompleteOilExercise(true));
+            }
+        }
+        else if (currentExercise == 4)
+        {
+            if (!turboExerciseCompleted && playerController.HasUsedTurbo)
+            {
+                turboExerciseCompleted = true;
+
+                Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Turbo activated successfully!");
+
+                StartCoroutine(CompleteTurboExercise());
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+
+
+    private IEnumerator CompleteExercise()
+    {
+        transitioning = true;
+
+        if (instructionText != null)
+        {
+            instructionText.text =
+                "¡Excelente! Cambio de carril completado.";
+        }
+
+        Debug.Log("Tutorial exercise completed!");
+
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(messageDelay);
+
+        if (currentExercise == 0)
+        {
+            StartExercise(1);
+        }
+        else if (currentExercise == 1)
+        {
+            StartExercise(2);
+        }
+    }
+
+
+
+    private IEnumerator CompleteFuelExercise()
+    {
+        transitioning = true;
+
+        playerController.SetForwardSpeed(0f);
+
+        UpdateUI(
+            "¡Excelente! Recuperaste combustible.",
+            "PASO 3 DE 5"
+        );
+
+        Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Fuel exercise completed!");
+
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(messageDelay);
+
+        playerController.canMove = false;
+        StartExercise(3);
+    }
+
+
+    private IEnumerator CompleteOilExercise(bool avoidedOil)
+    {
+        transitioning = true;
+
+        playerController.SetForwardSpeed(0f);
+        playerController.canMove = false;
+
+        if (avoidedOil)
+        {
+            UpdateUI(
+                "¡Excelente! Esquivaste la mancha de aceite.",
+                "PASO 4 DE 5"
+            );
+
+            Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Oil avoided successfully!");
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            UpdateUI(
+                "¡Cuidado! Tocaste el aceite. Inténtalo de nuevo.",
+                "PASO 4 DE 5"
+            );
+
+            Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Player touched oil. Retry required.");
+        }
+
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(messageDelay);
+
+
+        if (avoidedOil)
+        {
+            if (tutorialOilSpill != null)
+            {
+                tutorialOilSpill.SetActive(false);
+            }
+
+            Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Starting turbo exercise.");
+
+            StartExercise(4);
+        }
+
+        else
+        {
+            Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Restarting oil exercise.");
+
+            if (tutorialOilSpill != null)
+            {
+                tutorialOilSpill.SetActive(false);
+            }
+
+            playerController.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(
+                oilExerciseStartPosition,
+                oilExerciseStartRotation
+            );
+
+            if (oilDetector != null)
+            {
+                oilDetector.ResetDetection();
+            }
+
+            StartExercise(3);
+        }
+    }
+
+
+
+    private IEnumerator CompleteTurboExercise()
+    {
+        transitioning = true;
+
+        UpdateUI(
+            "¡Excelente! Activaste correctamente el propulsor.",
+            "PASO 5 DE 5"
+        );
+
+        Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Turbo exercise completed!");
+
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(messageDelay);
+
+        playerController.SetForwardSpeed(0f);
+        playerController.canMove = false;
+
+        currentExercise = 5;
+
+        if (instructionsPanel != null)
+        {
+            instructionsPanel.SetActive(false);
+        }
+
+        if (completionPanel != null)
+        {
+            completionPanel.SetActive(true);
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            Debug.LogError("TUTORIAL: Completion Panel is not assigned!");
+        }
+
+        Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Training completed.");
+    }
+
+
+    private void UpdateUI(string message, string progress)
+    {
+        if (instructionText != null)
+            instructionText.text = message;
+
+        if (progressText != null)
+            progressText.text = progress;
+    }
+
+    public void StartMainRace()
+    {
+        Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Starting main race.");
+
+        SceneManager.LoadScene("Gameplay");
+    }
+
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class TutorialOilDetector : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    private bool playerTouchedOil = false;
+
+    public bool PlayerTouchedOil
+    {
+        get { return playerTouchedOil; }
+    }
+
+    public void ResetDetection()
+    {
+        playerTouchedOil = false;
+    }
+
+    private void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
+    {
+        KartLaneController player =
+            other.GetComponentInParent<KartLaneController>();
+
+        if (player != null && player.CompareTag("Player"))
+        {
+            playerTouchedOil = true;
+
+            Debug.Log("TUTORIAL: Player touched the oil!");
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/DebugClick.cs b/Assets/Scripts/DebugClick.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fcb7961
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/DebugClick.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
+using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
+
+public class DebugClick : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    void Update()
+    {
+        // Revisa el clic con el sistema nuevo Y el viejo
+        bool clickNuevo = Mouse.current != null && Mouse.current.leftButton.wasPressedThisFrame;
+        bool clickViejo = Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0);
+
+        if (clickNuevo || clickViejo)
+        {
+            Debug.Log("<color=cyan>1. ¡El mouse HIZO CLIC en la pantalla!</color>");
+
+            if (EventSystem.current != null)
+            {
+                if (EventSystem.current.IsPointerOverGameObject())
+                {
+                    PointerEventData pointerData = new PointerEventData(EventSystem.current);
+                    pointerData.position = Mouse.current != null ? Mouse.current.position.ReadValue() : (Vector2)Input.mousePosition;
+
+                    var results = new System.Collections.Generic.List<RaycastResult>();
+                    EventSystem.current.RaycastAll(pointerData, results);
+
+                    if (results.Count > 0)
+                    {
+                        Debug.Log("<color=green>2. La UI detectó el clic sobre: " + results[0].gameObject.name + "</color>");
+                    }
+                }
+                else
+                {

    KartCamera
    
    A KartCamera script is added to give the camera dynamism.

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/KartCamera.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/KartCamera.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..174d0a6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/KartCamera.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,47 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class KartCamera : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Target References")]
+    [SerializeField] private Transform kartTarget;
+    [SerializeField] private KartLaneController kartController;
+
+    [Header("Follow Settings")]
+    [SerializeField] private Vector3 offset = new Vector3(0f, 3f, -5f);
+    [SerializeField] private float smoothSpeed = 5f;
+
+    [Header("Dynamic FOV Settings")]
+    [SerializeField] private Camera cam;
+    [SerializeField] private float minFOV = 60f;
+    [SerializeField] private float maxFOV = 80f;
+    [SerializeField] private float fovChangeSpeed = 3f;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        // Automatically fetch Camera component if unassigned
+        if (cam == null)
+        {
+            cam = GetComponent<Camera>();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void LateUpdate()
+    {
+        if (kartTarget == null) return;
+
+        // 1. Smooth position tracking using Lerp
+        Vector3 desiredPosition = kartTarget.position + offset;
+        transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(transform.position, desiredPosition, smoothSpeed * Time.deltaTime);
+
+        // 2. Adjust Camera FOV dynamically based on current kart speed
+        if (kartController != null && cam != null)
+        {
+            // Normalize speed value between 0.0 and 1.0
+            float speedRatio = kartController.currentForwardSpeed / 15f; 
+            float targetFOV = Mathf.Lerp(minFOV, maxFOV, speedRatio);
+
+            // Interpolate FOV smoothly
+            cam.fieldOfView = Mathf.Lerp(cam.fieldOfView, targetFOV, Time.deltaTime * fovChangeSpeed);
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/UI/MusicManager.cs b/Assets/Scripts/UI/MusicManager.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..86837cb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/UI/MusicManager.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,60 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
+
+public class MusicManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    public static MusicManager Instance;
+
+    public AudioClip menuMusic;
+    public AudioClip gameplayMusic;
+
+    private AudioSource audioSource;
+
+    private void Awake()
+    {
+        if (Instance == null)
+        {
+            Instance = this;
+            DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);
+
+            audioSource = GetComponent<AudioSource>();
+
+            SceneManager.sceneLoaded += OnSceneLoaded;
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            Destroy(gameObject);
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        PlayMusic(menuMusic);
+    }
+
+    void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
+    {
+        if (scene.name == "Gameplay")
+        {
+            PlayMusic(gameplayMusic);
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            PlayMusic(menuMusic);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void PlayMusic(AudioClip clip)
+    {
+        if (audioSource.clip == clip)
+            return;
+
+        audioSource.clip = clip;
+        audioSource.Play();
+    }
+
+    private void OnDestroy()
+    {
+        SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnSceneLoaded;
+    }
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,38 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class OilSpill : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Audio Settings")]
+    [SerializeField] private AudioSource audioSource;
+    [SerializeField] private AudioClip oilSound;
+
+    [Header("Settings")]
+    [SerializeField] private bool destroyOnStepped = false; // Por si quieres que la mancha desaparezca al pisarla
+
+    private bool hasBeenStepped = false;
+
+    private void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
+    {
+        // Verifica si lo que pisó la mancha es el Jugador o la CPU (usando Tags o un componente)
+        if (!hasBeenStepped && (other.CompareTag("Player") || other.CompareTag("CPU")))
+        {
+            hasBeenStepped = true;
+            PlayOilSound();
+
+            if (destroyOnStepped)
+            {
+                // Esconde el modelo visual y destruye el objeto después de que termine el audio
+                GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().enabled = false;
+                Destroy(gameObject, oilSound != null ? oilSound.length : 0.5f);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void PlayOilSound()
+    {
+        if (audioSource != null && oilSound != null)
+        {
+            audioSource.PlayOneShot(oilSound);
+        }
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644

+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Player/CharacterMaterialManager.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,47 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.UI;
+
+public class CharacterMaterialManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Kart Fronts")]
+    [SerializeField] private MeshRenderer playerFront;
+    [SerializeField] private MeshRenderer cpuFront;
+
+    [Header("HUD References")]
+    [SerializeField] private Image playerBar;
+    [SerializeField] private Image cpuBar;
+
+    [Header("Kart Materials")]
+    [SerializeField] private Material[] kartMaterials;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        ApplyMaterials();
+    }
+
+    private void ApplyMaterials()
+    {
+        if (kartMaterials == null || kartMaterials.Length < 8)
+        {
+            Debug.LogError("Please assign all 8 kart materials.");
+            return;
+        }
+
+        int playerIndex = GameData.PlayerSelection;
+        int cpuIndex = GameData.CpuSelection;
+
+        // Change kart colors
+        if (playerFront != null)
+            playerFront.material = kartMaterials[playerIndex];
+
+        if (cpuFront != null)
+            cpuFront.material = kartMaterials[cpuIndex];
+
+        // Change HUD colors
+        if (playerBar != null)
+            playerBar.color = kartMaterials[playerIndex].color;
+
+        if (cpuBar != null)
+            cpuBar.color = kartMaterials[cpuIndex].color;
+    }
+}

+using System.Collections;
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class CPUController : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Lane Configuration")]
+    [Tooltip("Horizontal distance between each lane center.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float laneOffset = 2f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Speed at which the kart transitions horizontally between lanes.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float laneChangeSpeed = 15f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Forward speed of the CPU kart.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float cpuSpeed = 6f;
+
+    [Header("Lane Limits (4 Lanes Setup)")]
+    [Tooltip("Minimum lane index (leftmost lane).")]
+    [SerializeField] private int minLane = -2;
+
+    [Tooltip("Maximum lane index (rightmost lane).")]
+    [SerializeField] private int maxLane = 1;
+
+    [Header("AI Control")]
+    [Tooltip("Allows the CPU kart to move.")]
+    public bool canMove = false;
+
+    [Tooltip("How often the CPU chooses a new lane.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float laneChangeInterval = 2f;
+
+    [Tooltip("How far ahead the CPU scans for objects.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float scanDistance = 20f;
+
+    [Header("Lane Weights")]
+    [Tooltip("Weight for an empty lane.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float emptyLaneWeight = 10f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Weight for a lane with a fuel can.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float fuelCanWeight = 50f;
+
+    [Tooltip("Weight for a lane with oil.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float oilSpillWeight = 2f;
+
+    [Header("Oil Effect")]
+    [SerializeField] private float oilSlowMultiplier = 0.5f;
+    [SerializeField] private float oilSlowDuration = 2f;
+
+    [Header("Start Lane")]
+    [Tooltip("Initial lane of the CPU kart. Example: -1, 0, 1, 2.")]
+    [SerializeField] private int startLane = 1;
+
+    private int currentLane;
+    private int targetLane;
+    private float laneChangeTimer;
+    private float startXPosition;
+
+    private float currentForwardSpeed;
+    private KartFuelSystem fuelSystem;
+    private bool isSliding = false;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        fuelSystem = GetComponent<KartFuelSystem>();
+
+        // Save the X position where this kart starts.
+        startXPosition = transform.position.x;
+
+        // Start in the assigned lane.
+        currentLane = Mathf.Clamp(startLane, minLane, maxLane);
+        targetLane = currentLane;
+
+        // Set the initial forward speed.
+        currentForwardSpeed = cpuSpeed;
+
+        // Start the lane decision timer.
+        laneChangeTimer = laneChangeInterval;
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        // If movement is blocked, do nothing.
+        if (!canMove)
+            return;
+
+        // If the GameManager does not exist, do nothing.
+        if (GameManager.Instance == null)
+            return;
+
+        // The CPU only moves during its own turn.
+        if (GameManager.Instance.gameState != GameManager.GameState.CPUTurn)
+            return;
+
+        // If the kart has no fuel, it cannot move.
+        if (fuelSystem != null && fuelSystem.IsOutOfFuel)
+            return;
+
+        MoveCPU();
+
+        laneChangeTimer -= Time.deltaTime;
+        if (laneChangeTimer <= 0f)
+        {
+            ChooseLane();
+            laneChangeTimer = laneChangeInterval;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Moves the kart forward and smoothly to the target lane.
+    private void MoveCPU()
+    {
+        float targetX = GetLanePosition(targetLane);
+
+        Vector3 currentPos = transform.position;
+
+        // Smooth horizontal lane change.
+        float newX = Mathf.MoveTowards(
+            currentPos.x,
+            targetX,
+            laneChangeSpeed * Time.deltaTime
+        );
+
+        // Forward movement, same style as the player's kart.
+        float newZ = currentPos.z + (currentForwardSpeed * Time.deltaTime);
+
+        // Prevent leaving the track on the sides.
+        float minX = GetLanePosition(minLane);
+        float maxX = GetLanePosition(maxLane);
+        newX = Mathf.Clamp(newX, minX, maxX);
+
+        transform.position = new Vector3(newX, currentPos.y, newZ);
+
+        // When it reaches the lane, update the current lane.
+        if (Mathf.Abs(transform.position.x - targetX) < 0.05f)
+        {
+            currentLane = targetLane;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Chooses a new lane using weights based on nearby objects.
+    private void ChooseLane()
+    {
+        // It can only move one lane to the left or to the right.
+        int[] possibleLanes;
+
+        if (currentLane <= minLane)
+        {
+            possibleLanes = new int[] { currentLane + 1 };
+        }
+        else if (currentLane >= maxLane)
+        {
+            possibleLanes = new int[] { currentLane - 1 };
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            possibleLanes = new int[] { currentLane - 1, currentLane + 1 };
+        }
+
+        // Choose one of the adjacent lanes.
+        targetLane = possibleLanes[Random.Range(0, possibleLanes.Length)];
+    }
+
+    // Returns a weight for a lane based on what the CPU detects ahead.
+    private float EvaluateLaneWeight(int lane)
+    {
+        float targetX = GetLanePosition(lane);
+
+        Vector3 rayOrigin = new Vector3(
+            targetX,
+            transform.position.y + 0.5f,
+            transform.position.z
+        );
+
+        if (Physics.Raycast(rayOrigin, Vector3.forward, out RaycastHit hit, scanDistance))
+        {
+            if (hit.collider.CompareTag("FuelCan"))
+                return fuelCanWeight;
+
+            if (hit.collider.CompareTag("OilSpill"))
+                return oilSpillWeight;
+        }
+
+        return emptyLaneWeight;
+    }
+
+    // Picks one lane based on the weights array.
+    private int GetWeightedRandomLane(float[] weights)
+    {
+        float totalWeight = 0f;
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < weights.Length; i++)
+            totalWeight += weights[i];
+
+        if (totalWeight <= 0f)
+        {
+            // Fallback: choose any lane except the current one.
+            int fallbackLane = currentLane;
+            while (fallbackLane == currentLane)
+            {
+                fallbackLane = Random.Range(minLane, maxLane + 1);
+            }
+            return fallbackLane - minLane;
+        }
+
+        float randomValue = Random.Range(0f, totalWeight);
+        float cumulativeWeight = 0f;
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < weights.Length; i++)
+        {
+            cumulativeWeight += weights[i];
+            if (randomValue <= cumulativeWeight)
+                return i;
+        }
+
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // Gets the X position for a given lane.
+    private float GetLanePosition(int lane)
+    {
+        return startXPosition + (lane * laneOffset);
+    }
+
+    private void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
+    {
+       
+        Debug.Log(
+            "The CPU came into contact with: "
+            + other.gameObject.name
+            + " | Tag: "
+            + other.gameObject.tag
+        );
+
+
+        // Comprueba si el objeto es un barril de combustible.
+        if (other.CompareTag("Fuel"))
+        {
+            Debug.Log(
+                "The CPU picked up a fuel barrel."
+            );
+
+
+            // Checks if the object is a fuel barrel..
+            if (fuelSystem != null)
+            {
+                Debug.Log(
+                    "Increasing fuel."
+                );
+
+                fuelSystem.AddFuel();
+            }
+
+
+
+            // Deactivates the barrel after picking it up.
+            other.gameObject.SetActive(false);
+
+
+            Debug.Log(
+                "Barrel deactivated."
+            );
+
+
+            return;
+        }
+
+
+        // Checks if the object is an oil slick.
+        if (other.CompareTag("Oil"))
+        {
+            Debug.Log(
+                "The CPU stepped on an oil slick."
+            );
+
+
+            // Reduces fuel consumption.
+            if (fuelSystem != null)
+            {
+                Debug.Log(
+                    "Reducing fuel in favor of oil."
+                );
+
+                fuelSystem.ReduceFuelFromOil();
+            }
+
+
+
+            // Applies the speed reduction.
+            ApplyOilSlow(
+                oilSlowDuration
+            );
+
+
+            Debug.Log(
+                "Effect of applied oil."
+            );
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Applies a temporary speed reduction.
+    private void ApplyOilSlow(float duration)
+    {
+        if (!isSliding)
+        {
+            StartCoroutine(OilSlowRoutine(duration));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private IEnumerator OilSlowRoutine(float duration)
+    {
+        isSliding = true;
+
+        float originalSpeed = currentForwardSpeed;
+        currentForwardSpeed = cpuSpeed * oilSlowMultiplier;
+
+        Debug.Log("CPU slipped on oil. Speed reduced.");
+
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(duration);
+
+        if (fuelSystem == null || !fuelSystem.IsOutOfFuel)
+        {
+            currentForwardSpeed = cpuSpeed;
+        }
+
+        isSliding = false;
+        Debug.Log("CPU speed recovered.");
+    }
+
+    
+}

+using TMPro;
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.UI;
+
+public class UIManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("HUD References")]
+    [SerializeField] private Image fuelBarFill;
+    [SerializeField] private TMP_Text distanceText;
+
+    [Header("Gameplay References")]
+    [SerializeField] private KartFuelSystem playerFuelSystem;
+    [SerializeField] private DistanceTracker playerDistanceTracker;
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        UpdateFuelBar();
+        UpdateDistance();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Updates the fuel bar based on the player's remaining fuel.
+    /// </summary>
+    private void UpdateFuelBar()
+    {
+        if (playerFuelSystem == null || fuelBarFill == null)
+            return;
+
+        fuelBarFill.fillAmount = playerFuelSystem.GetFuelNormalized();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Updates the distance text in real time.
+    /// </summary>
+    private void UpdateDistance()
+    {
+        if (playerDistanceTracker == null || distanceText == null)
+            return;
+
+        distanceText.text = $"{playerDistanceTracker.currentDistance:F1} YDS";
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Managers/CharacterSelectionManager.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Managers/CharacterSelectionManager.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..47b22ff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Managers/CharacterSelectionManager.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,171 @@
+using System.Collections;
+using UnityEngine;
+using UnityEngine.UI;
+using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
+
+public class CharacterSelectionManager : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Selection Borders")]
+    [SerializeField] private RectTransform playerBorder;
+    [SerializeField] private RectTransform cpuBorder;
+
+    [Header("Color Points")]
+    [SerializeField] private Transform[] colorPoints;
+
+    [Header("Locked Overlays")]
+    [SerializeField] private GameObject[] lockOverlays;
+
+    [Header("CPU Settings")]
+    [SerializeField] private float cpuMoveSpeed = 0.08f;
+    [SerializeField] private float cpuThinkingTime = 1.2f;
+
+
+    private int currentSelection = 0;
+
+    private bool playerConfirmed = false;
+    private bool inputEnabled = true;
+
+    private int playerSelection = -1;
+    private int cpuSelection = -1;
+    private int cpuCurrentIndex = 0;
+
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        cpuBorder.gameObject.SetActive(false);
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < lockOverlays.Length; i++)
+        {
+            lockOverlays[i].SetActive(false);
+        }
+
+        UpdatePlayerBorder();
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        if (!inputEnabled)
+            return;
+
+        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.RightArrow))
+        {
+            MoveRight();
+        }
+
+        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.LeftArrow))
+        {
+            MoveLeft();
+        }
+
+        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.UpArrow))
+        {
+            MoveUp();
+        }
+
+        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.DownArrow))
+        {
+            MoveDown();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void MoveRight()
+    {
+        if (currentSelection % 4 < 3)
+        {
+            currentSelection++;
+            UpdatePlayerBorder();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void MoveLeft()
+    {
+        if (currentSelection % 4 > 0)
+        {
+            currentSelection--;
+            UpdatePlayerBorder();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void MoveUp()
+    {
+        Debug.Log("MoveUp ejecutado");
+
+        if (currentSelection >= 4)
+        {
+            currentSelection -= 4;
+            UpdatePlayerBorder();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void MoveDown()
+    {
+        Debug.Log("MoveDown ejecutado");
+
+        if (currentSelection < 4)
+        {
+            currentSelection += 4;
+            UpdatePlayerBorder();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void UpdatePlayerBorder()
+    {
+        Debug.Log("Current Selection: " + currentSelection);
+
+        playerBorder.position = colorPoints[currentSelection].position;
+    }
+
+    public void ConfirmSelection()
+    {
+        if (playerConfirmed)
+            return;
+
+        playerConfirmed = true;
+        inputEnabled = false;
+
+        playerSelection = currentSelection;
+
+        lockOverlays[playerSelection].SetActive(true);
+
+        cpuBorder.gameObject.SetActive(true);
+
+        StartCoroutine(CPUSelectionRoutine());
+    }
+    private IEnumerator CPUSelectionRoutine()
+    {
+        do
+        {
+            cpuSelection = Random.Range(0, colorPoints.Length);
+
+        } while (cpuSelection == playerSelection);
+
+        cpuCurrentIndex = 0;
+
+        float timer = 0f;
+
+        while (timer < cpuThinkingTime)
+        {
+            cpuBorder.position = colorPoints[cpuCurrentIndex].position;
+
+            cpuCurrentIndex++;
+
+            if (cpuCurrentIndex >= colorPoints.Length)
+                cpuCurrentIndex = 0;
+
+            timer += cpuMoveSpeed;
+
+            yield return new WaitForSeconds(cpuMoveSpeed);
+        }
+
+        cpuBorder.position = colorPoints[cpuSelection].position;
+        
+        lockOverlays[cpuSelection].SetActive(true);
+
+        GameData.PlayerSelection = playerSelection;
+        GameData.CpuSelection = cpuSelection;
+
+        yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.8f);
+
+        SceneManager.LoadScene("Gameplay");
+    }
+}

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/DestroyAfterDistance.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/DestroyAfterDistance.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c504e9e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/DestroyAfterDistance.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,35 @@
+using UnityEngine;
+
+public class DestroyAfterDistance : MonoBehaviour
+{
+    [Header("Settings")]
+    [Tooltip("Reference to the player/kart transform.")]
+    [SerializeField] private Transform playerTransform;
+
+    [Tooltip("Distance behind the player at which this object will be destroyed.")]
+    [SerializeField] private float destroyDistanceBehind = 15f;
+
+    private void Start()
+    {
+        // Automatically find the player by tag if not assigned in Inspector
+        if (playerTransform == null)
+        {
+            GameObject player = GameObject.FindGameObjectWithTag("Player");
+            if (player != null)
+            {
+                playerTransform = player.transform;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void Update()
+    {
+        if (playerTransform == null) return;
+
+        // Check if the obstacle is far enough behind the player's Z position
+        if (playerTransform.position.z - transform.position.z > destroyDistanceBehind)
+        {
+            Destroy(gameObject);
+        }
+    }
+}

commit 13e36d91744ac322e4965be8222aa0d0e84a67b1
Author:     IvanC117 <castellanosivan2020@outlook.com>

     The GameManager and DistanceTracker scripts are created.
    
    The GameManager script controls the flow of the match—such as the game state (Player Turn, CPU Turn, Waiting, Finished)—toggles the visibility of the karts, and compares the distances traveled by the Player and the CPU to determine the winner or declare a draw.
    
    DistanceTracker automatically locates the "Start" and "Finish" objects upon startup, measures the distance along the Z-axis traveled by the kart between the start and finish lines, rounds it to two decimal places, and sends it to the game manager.

diff --git a/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/DistanceTracker.cs b/Assets/Scripts/Gameplay/DistanceTracker.cs
